# Dataset gap report — wiki sync (P6-S0)

Generated by `npm run pipeline:build` — do not edit by hand. Terraria 1.4.5.8; data as of 2026-10-15T18:21:41.949Z; Module:Iteminfo/data rev 1023241 (synced to 1.4.5.7).

## Weapons — why gatedBy / stage are Unknown (§7b blockers)

gatedBy derived: 154/488 (P2-S2: 0/488, before the boss registry and progression graph existed). A route is derivable only when every input in its chain is verified: boss drops (incl. treasure bags) now gate on their boss node, conditions with satisfiedByNode on their node, transitively reduced over the required-gate DAG; since the B70 P4-prep slice (AMENDMENTS-014 B68 / AMENDMENTS-015 B78) reviewed vendor routes, chests / orbs and a curated gatherable base-material registry are inputs too (see the P4-prep sections below); enemy and event-boss stages, fishing (crates) and unreviewed vendors stay Unknown, so chains through them stay underivable. Derived weapons by stage: early-prehm 90, late-prehm 11, early-hm 12, mid-hm 31, endgame 10. Each weapon below is Unknown → never "obtainable now" (A3 safe direction).

| Blockers met on every route | Weapons | Examples |
|---|---:|---|
| drop-source-ungated | 125 | Harpoon; Water Bolt; Flamelash |
| alternative-route-unverified + drop-source-ungated + vendor-conditions-unknown | 91 | Iron Broadsword; Iron Shortsword; Demon Bow |
| vendor-availability-unknown | 26 | Cannon; Red Rocket; Green Rocket |
| alternative-route-unverified + drop-source-ungated | 20 | Vilethorn; Musket; Magic Missile |
| no-known-route | 17 | Bladed Glove; Flairoon; Cascade |
| fishing-route-ungated | 9 | Purple Clubberfish; Obsidian Swordfish; Swordfish |
| drop-conditions-unknown | 8 | Flower of Fire; Magic Dagger; Unholy Trident |
| vendor-conditions-unknown | 6 | Mushroom Spear; Land Mine; Leather Whip |
| alternative-route-unverified + drop-conditions-unknown + drop-source-ungated + vendor-conditions-unknown | 5 | Night's Edge; True Night's Edge; Terra Blade |
| drop-conditions-unknown + drop-source-ungated | 4 | Paintball Gun; Stylish Scissors; Vampire Frog Staff |
| drop-source-ungated + vendor-conditions-unknown | 4 | Wet Bomb; Lava Bomb; Honey Bomb |
| drop-source-ungated + vendor-availability-unknown | 3 | Holy Water; Pirate Staff; Happy Grenade |
| drop-conditions-unknown + vendor-availability-unknown | 3 | Code 1; Terragrim; Gray Zapinator |
| alternative-route-unverified + drop-source-ungated + vendor-availability-unknown | 2 | Bone; Spinal Tap |
| alternative-route-unverified + drop-conditions-unknown + drop-source-ungated | 2 | Aqua Scepter; Slime Staff |
| alternative-route-unverified + vendor-availability-unknown | 2 | Unholy Water; Blood Water |
| alternative-route-unverified + drop-conditions-unknown | 2 | Grenade Launcher; Rainbow Gun |
| drop-source-ungated + no-known-route | 2 | Trusty Cattiva; Trusty Foxparks |
| alternative-route-unverified + drop-source-ungated + no-known-route + vendor-conditions-unknown | 1 | Flymeal |
| alternative-route-unverified + drop-source-ungated + vendor-availability-unknown + vendor-conditions-unknown | 1 | Mushroom Staff |
| acquisition-withheld | 1 | Pretty Mirror |

- **no-known-route**: an input item has no recipe, drop, vendor, gatherable or fishing route in the datasets (an unmodeled source: event loot, NPC gifts, Extractinator output, …)
- **fishing-route-ungated**: a fishing route (the "Fishing catches" article, AMENDMENTS-004 B20) exists, but whether its pond / biome / height is reachable before a boss is not reviewed — fishing never counts as a verified gate (P6-S0)
- **drop-source-ungated**: a drop comes from an enemy, an event boss or a container outside the reviewed container registry (Enemy.stage Unknown; event bosses are progression markers, not Boss records; crates / lock boxes / bags are not reviewed)
- **boss-attribution-pending**: a boss drop has no verified boss → progression-node attribution (source.bossId Unknown: the row's NPC ids disagree with the registry boss)
- **drop-conditions-unknown**: a drop lists its conditions as Unknown
- **vendor-conditions-unknown**: a purchase route exists but the item's row in the NPC shop table is missing or its Availability text is not a reviewed condition (an OR, an item in the inventory, a season, a Bestiary / golf threshold …)
- **progression-graph-pending**: a gate maps to a progression node that is not in the dataset, so the stage cannot be computed
- **acquisition-withheld**: the item's acquisition is withheld because wiki sources conflict (AMENDMENTS-004 B19)
- **alternative-route-unverified**: one route is derivable, but another route is not, and it could be the less gated one — the minimum over routes is unknown (§7b)
- **vendor-availability-unknown**: a purchase route's vendor NPC has no honestly derivable availability (several alternative bosses, a count of other NPCs, a random visit — overlays/vendors.json)
- **condition-alternatives**: the least-gated routes need different world / biome / time conditions and no single condition list is their minimum (an either-or GatedBy cannot express)
- **cycle**: every route needs the item itself (recipe cycle)

## Bosses — stats per difficulty (Cargo NPCs) and why the rest is Unknown

hp / defense / contact damage of the representative Cargo NPCs row (pipeline/overlays/bosses.json `primary`) — the NPC's BASE stats as in the article's first infobox. Later-phase changes shown only in secondary infoboxes (e.g. second-form defense of Eye of Cthulhu, Plantera, Empress of Light) are not modeled. Bosses whose simultaneous parts have different stats (Wall of Flesh mouth/eyes, segments, Twins, Golem, Moon Lord) are Unknown rather than represented by one part. "none" = the wiki gives 0 contact damage (not applicable); Unknown = absent + listed in _prov.unverifiedFields.

## Boss pages — P5 curated fields and the E_BOSS_CURATION gate (AMENDMENTS-001 A1)

Bosses with a page (src/schemas/bossPages.ts = manifest.bossPages): king-slime, eye-of-cthulhu, eater-of-worlds, brain-of-cthulhu, queen-bee, skeletron, deerclops, wall-of-flesh, queen-slime, the-twins, the-destroyer, skeletron-prime, plantera, golem, empress-of-light, duke-fishron, lunatic-cultist, moon-lord. Each must carry ≥ 1 quoted attack-pattern tag and, for prepChecklist / strategies / suggestedEquipment, ≥ 1 curated entry or a declared honest gap (Unknown + the reason below). Every strategy is a verbatim quote of its cited page; every checklist line is short editorial wording backed by a quote checked on its page; outcome-promising quotes are rejected (pipeline/merge/overlays.ts OUTCOME_PROMISE).

Suggested gear: whole-field Unknown while no weapon passes E_SUGGESTION_GATING for the boss; otherwise authored entries (each backed by a quote recommending its weapons) next to per-class Unknowns for classes with no weapon both quoted and verified obtainable before the fight (P5-S3). A per-class Unknown is stale — the build fails — once a weapon of that class passing the gate is named in the boss's own quoted strategies.

Declared gaps (rendered Unknown on the page, with the reason):

- boss:king-slime suggestedEquipment.ranged: King Slime: No ranged weapon the wiki recommends for this fight is verified as obtainable before it: the weapons its tips name come from sources whose gating the data cannot derive (enemy drops, fishing, vendors or containers not reviewed for gating) or from fights that are not a required step before this one. Gear that might not be available is not suggested, so ranged gear stays Unknown; the ranged tips quote the wiki's own advice.
- boss:eye-of-cthulhu suggestedEquipment.melee: Eye of Cthulhu: No melee weapon the wiki recommends for this fight is verified as obtainable before it: the weapons its tips name come from sources whose gating the data cannot derive (enemy drops, fishing, vendors or containers not reviewed for gating) or from fights that are not a required step before this one. Gear that might not be available is not suggested, so melee gear stays Unknown; the melee tips quote the wiki's own advice.

## Stage-transition gates (AMENDMENTS-005 B22)

§7b stage = max over the gating nodes of (unlocksStage ?? stage). Stage-transition nodes: wall-of-flesh (late-prehm → unlocks early-hm) — each validated against a required-gate edge into a milestone of that stage. Mechanical bosses set none: one mech boss does not open mid-hm (the all-mechanical-bosses milestone does); Plantera / Golem are mid-hm nodes whose drops are mid-hm already.

Derived weapon stages without unlocksStage: early-prehm 90, late-prehm 15, early-hm 8, mid-hm 31, endgame 10. With it (emitted): early-prehm 90, late-prehm 11, early-hm 12, mid-hm 31, endgame 10.

Records whose stage moved: Breaker Blade (weapon, late-prehm → early-hm; gated by wall-of-flesh); Clockwork Assault Rifle (weapon, late-prehm → early-hm; gated by wall-of-flesh); Laser Rifle (weapon, late-prehm → early-hm; gated by wall-of-flesh); Firecracker (weapon, late-prehm → early-hm; gated by wall-of-flesh); Pwnhammer (item, late-prehm → early-hm; gated by wall-of-flesh); Wall of Flesh Trophy (item, late-prehm → early-hm; gated by wall-of-flesh); Wall of Flesh Mask (item, late-prehm → early-hm; gated by wall-of-flesh); Treasure Bag (Wall of Flesh) (item, late-prehm → early-hm; gated by wall-of-flesh); Goat Skull (item, late-prehm → early-hm; gated by wall-of-flesh); Wall of Flesh Relic (item, late-prehm → early-hm; gated by wall-of-flesh).

## Ammo — identification and count reconciliation (P2-S7)

| Step | Count |
|---|---:|
| Module:Iteminfo/data items with a non-zero `ammo` (AmmoID) | 80 |
| Cargo Items rows whose infobox type contains "ammunition" | 80 |
| **= ammo records** | **80** |
|   ammo items (items.detail.json, category "ammo") | 79 |
|   weapons that are also ammo (ammoStats on the weapon record: Snowball) | 1 |
| Iteminfo ammo items without an emitted record | 0 |

**Wiki category tree** — Category:Ammunition items: 8 categories (Arrows, Bullets, Coins, Darts, Flares, Rockets, Sands), 87 distinct articles.

| Category-tree articles | Count |
|---|---:|
| articles in the tree | 87 |
| − articles hosting ≥1 emitted ammo | 56 |
| − redirects (category kept on a redirect page; the target article is classified on its own) | 26 |
| − articles whose only ammo rows are excluded (no item ID / platform-exclusive) | 0 |
| − articles whose only matching rows are out of scope by rule (P2-S8: vanity accessories → item corpus P6-S0) | 0 |
| − item articles whose infobox type is not "ammo" (tools etc.) | 1 |
| − articles without an item infobox (list / overview / mechanic pages) | 4 |
| − member titles that do not resolve to an existing page | 0 |
| = check: 87 classified | OK |
| ammo records hosted by the 56 ammo articles | 80 |
| + ammo records whose article is outside the tree | 0 |
| **= ammo records** | **80** (reconciles) |

- Redirects: Blue Flare → Flares; Copper Coin → Coins; Copper Coin/cs → Coins/cs; Crimsand Block → Sand Blocks; Cursed Flare → Flares; Dry Rocket → Liquid rockets; Ebonsand Block → Sand Blocks; Flare → Flares; Gold Coin → Coins; Gold Coin/cs → Coins/cs; Honey Rocket → Liquid rockets; Lava Rocket → Liquid rockets; Pearlsand Block → Sand Blocks; Platinum Coin → Coins; Platinum Coin/cs → Coins/cs; Rainbow Flare → Flares; Rocket I → Rockets; Rocket II → Rockets; Rocket III → Rockets; Rocket IV → Rockets; Sand Block → Sand Blocks; Shimmer Flare → Flares; Silver Coin → Coins; Silver Coin/cs → Coins/cs; Solution → Solutions; Wet Rocket → Liquid rockets
- Articles without an item infobox (list / localized pages): Ammunition items; Arrows; Bullets; Darts
- Non-ammo item articles in the tree: Wire (by infobox type: crafting material + mechanism 1)
- Ammo records outside the tree: none

**Ammo classes** (AmmoID representative item; the 17 weapon classes are all matched — §7a-10 hard check): acorn 1, ale 1, candy-corn 1, copper-coin 4, explosive-jack-o-lantern 1, fallen-star 1, flare 6, gel 1, green-solution 8, musket-ball 16, nail 1, rocket-i 12, sand-block 4, seed 5, snowball 1, stake 1, stynger-bolt 1, wooden-arrow 15.

## Weapon Lab — archetype params and coverage (P3-S0)

Rules: pipeline/merge/labParams.ts (header = the tooling doc). Param semantics are binding as documented in src/sim/README.md ("Cadence" and "Param semantics"; AMENDMENTS-008 B37): pierce = extra dummies after the first; projectilesPerUse = projectiles per activation (bursts come from useTime < useAnimation); ammo speed only from the ammo's velocityBonusTps / velocityMultiplier; magic-bolt speed = params.projectile.velocityTps. Family = the wiki category of the weapon's article (swing-melee: Broadswords; thrust: Shortswords + Spears; bow-gun: Bows + Repeaters + Guns; magic-bolt: Wands + Magic guns + Spell books; returning: Boomerangs + Flails + Yoyos; whip: Whips; minion-sentry: Minion summon items + Sentry summon items; explosive-aoe: Explosives + Launchers; piercing-beam: no wiki category — the held-beam data signal). "Derived" = every param computed from the data modules / sprite metadata and the article screen found nothing the params cannot express; "overlay" = the screen flagged the weapon and a reviewed entry in pipeline/overlays/lab-params.json (verbatim quotes, checked against the cached article revision) decided it; "downgraded" = params were assigned but a core field the sim needs is unverified, so the validate stage's §2.1-3 rule (incl. AMENDMENTS-008 B36) emits a limitation. Every emitted archetype weapon passed src/sim assessWeapon (build-time guard contract).

Families since the remaining-archetype slice: returning (Boomerangs + Flails + Yoyos), whip (Whips), minion-sentry (Minion + Sentry summon items), explosive-aoe (Explosives + Launchers) and piercing-beam (no wiki category — the held-beam data signal: held magic/ranged weapons outside every category family). Their sim interpreters have shipped with the Lab — returning + piercing-beam (P3-S6/S7), minion-sentry + whip (P3-S9/S10), explosive-aoe (P3-S11) — so every schema archetype is in `IMPLEMENTED_ARCHETYPES` and no weapon is "params complete, pending interpreter" any more (that column is kept for the record; AMENDMENTS-013 B61 flipped each family as it shipped). A member whose params are complete is emitted simulable, unless a core field the sim needs is unverified (then the validate stage downgrades it, visibly).

| Family (archetype) | Weapons | Derived | Overlay (reviewed) | Params complete, pending interpreter (core field unverified) | Downgraded at validate | Limitation (of which: a param missing) | **Simulable** | Simulable % |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| swing-melee | 75 | 22 | 15 | 0 (0) | 0 | 38 (0) | **37** | 49.3% |
| thrust | 31 | 5 | 13 | 0 (0) | 0 | 13 (0) | **18** | 58.1% |
| bow-gun | 67 | 27 | 11 | 0 (0) | 0 | 29 (0) | **38** | 56.7% |
| magic-bolt | 62 | 0 | 3 | 0 (0) | 0 | 59 (0) | **3** | 4.8% |
| returning | 51 | 0 | 22 | 0 (0) | 0 | 29 (0) | **22** | 43.1% |
| whip | 18 | 0 | 6 | 0 (0) | 0 | 12 (0) | **6** | 33.3% |
| minion-sentry | 46 | 0 | 10 | 0 (0) | 0 | 36 (7) | **10** | 21.7% |
| explosive-aoe | 27 | 0 | 15 | 0 (0) | 0 | 12 (0) | **15** | 55.6% |
| piercing-beam | 3 | 0 | 0 | 0 (0) | 0 | 3 (0) | **0** | 0.0% |
| **all 9 families** | 380 | 54 | 95 | 0 (0) | 0 | 231 (7) | **149** | 39.2% |
| all weapons (A3 MVP target ≥ 60% at P3-S13) | 488 | | | | | | **149** | 30.5% |
| weapons in no family (default limitation card) | 108 | | | | | | | |

| Family | Limitation reason | Weapons |
|---|---|---:|
| swing-melee | reviewed: the article documents behavior the archetype params cannot express (overlay) | 38 |
| thrust | reviewed: the article documents behavior the archetype params cannot express (overlay) | 11 |
| thrust | game data is not a plain stab (use style / held use / projectile is not the stab) | 2 |
| bow-gun | reviewed: the article documents behavior the archetype params cannot express (overlay) | 29 |
| magic-bolt | reviewed: the article documents behavior the archetype params cannot express (overlay) | 59 |
| returning | reviewed: the article documents behavior the archetype params cannot express (overlay) | 19 |
| returning | thrown flail (held use spins it around the player) — the returning params cannot express the spin | 10 |
| whip | reviewed: the article documents behavior the archetype params cannot express (overlay) | 12 |
| minion-sentry | reviewed: the article documents behavior the archetype params cannot express (overlay) | 29 |
| minion-sentry | a required param is stated nowhere on the wiki (or needs a convention ruling) — missing, never guessed | 7 |
| explosive-aoe | fires ammunition (launcher): the blast comes from the ammo, which explosive params do not carry | 10 |
| explosive-aoe | reviewed: the article documents behavior the archetype params cannot express (overlay) | 2 |
| piercing-beam | reviewed: the article documents behavior the archetype params cannot express (overlay) | 3 |

**AMENDMENTS-008 B36** (simRequiredWeaponFields += useAnimationTicks for every archetype, + autoReuse for swing-melee / thrust / returning): of 149 weapons given params, 0 are downgraded by the validate rule; 0 of them only because of the B36 fields (none). Weapons in the whole dataset with an unverified B36 field: Inferno Fork (autoReuse; magic-bolt → limitation); Starlight (autoReuse; thrust → limitation) — none of them had params to lose (a thrust limitation and a reviewed magic limitation), so B36 changes no emitted record in this build; it now guarantees that any future params on them are downgraded with a proper limitation card. B36 adds requirements only, so it upgrades nothing; the slice's upgrades are the 149 weapons whose labBehavior went from the P2 default limitation to a simulable archetype. Guard-only refusals turned into limitations at merge time: none.

**Coverage expansion (AMENDMENTS-010 B48; AMENDMENTS-012 B56 stated thrust ranges: thrust +9)** — simulable vs the P3-S0 baseline (swing-melee 35 · thrust 0 · bow-gun 34 · magic-bolt 2 = 71): swing-melee **37** (+2) · thrust **18** (+18) · bow-gun **38** (+4) · magic-bolt **3** (+1) = **96** (+25). Every P3-S0 archetype weapon kept its params; B49 adds `_prov.modeledFields` to the convention-derived ones.

**Per-projectile damage multipliers (B48(b))**: `ProjectileSpec.damageMultiplierPct` is supported end to end (schema, sim rounding ⌊damage × pct / 100⌋ as in the wiki's own figures, quote-checked overlay residual incl. a "(N base damage)" cross-check). Emitted on 0 weapons: every family weapon whose article states a per-projectile percentage also has behavior the Lab cannot express, so it stays a reviewed limitation — True Night's Edge, Sapphire Staff, Ruby Staff, Mushroom Spear, Chlorophyte Saber, Golem Fist, Ice Sickle, Obsidian Swordfish, Anchor, Amber Staff, Storm Spear, Sergeant United Shield, Mushroom Staff.

**Article screen** (high recall; a flag only routes a weapon to review, it never sets a value): projectile — fires or creates projectiles (swing-melee, thrust); extra-projectile — fires or creates projectiles (returning, whip, explosive-aoe); multi-projectile — several projectiles per shot (bow-gun, magic-bolt, returning); spread — spread or randomized aim (bow-gun, magic-bolt); conversion — converts or replaces its ammunition (bow-gun, magic-bolt); homing — homing (bow-gun, magic-bolt, returning, whip, explosive-aoe, piercing-beam); bounce — bouncing (bow-gun, magic-bolt, returning, explosive-aoe); pierce — piercing (bow-gun, magic-bolt); cursor — steered toward the cursor (returning); on-hit — extra effects on hit (swing-melee, thrust, bow-gun, magic-bolt, returning, whip, minion-sentry, explosive-aoe, piercing-beam); spawns — creates other projectiles or entities (swing-melee, thrust, bow-gun, magic-bolt, returning, whip, explosive-aoe, piercing-beam); conditional-damage — damage that depends on conditions (swing-melee, thrust, bow-gun, magic-bolt, returning, whip, minion-sentry, explosive-aoe, piercing-beam); motion — non-straight projectile motion (bow-gun, magic-bolt); special-use — charged, held or alternate use (swing-melee, thrust, bow-gun, magic-bolt, whip, minion-sentry, explosive-aoe); the tooltip is screened with the same rules; plus data flags: shoots-projectile (Iteminfo shoot > 0, melee), infobox-projectile, inflicts-debuff (Cargo debuffs / infobox buff), burst (useTime < useAnimation). Hard (non-overridable) data rules: use style / class / ammo / mana / channel signals per archetype; thrust: the Iteminfo projectile must be the stab itself (Projectileinfo name = weapon name); the reach is the range the article states (AMENDMENTS-012 B56) or, where none is stated, the B46 convention; a debuff with damage over time must be derivable from the infobox debuff rows (B48(c)). Since B48(a) Iteminfo reuseDelay is part of the sim cadence (no longer a limitation). Flag counts among family weapons: on-hit 241, conditional-damage 161, spawns 151, inflicts-debuff 91, multi-projectile 86, infobox-projectile 70, pierce 68, motion 41, bounce 39, projectile 37, special-use 36, spread 31, conversion 30, homing 28, shoots-projectile 27, extra-projectile 22, burst 13.

**Convention rulings applied (AMENDMENTS-013 B63)**: each param a ruling supplies is MODELED — recorded in the decision's `modeled` list (src/schemas MODELING_CONVENTIONS; a path two conventions share is qualified `<path>#<convention id>`), emitted as `_prov.modeledFields` and labeled by the sim's approximations registry (AssumptionsTip) once the archetype is simulated — never presented as game data. "Applied" counts every family member whose recorded param the convention supplied (reviewed limitations included); "params complete" = those now complete (pending their interpreter). **Schema deltas surfaced (not applied; for the schema owner)**: (S-1) returning params carry no count of projectiles that may be out at once — Bananarang (10), Trimarang (3), Light Disc (6) and Possessed Hatchet (unlimited) are reviewed limitations for it, while the Boomerangs article states "Most boomerangs cannot be fired again before returning to the player" (the interpreter's default); (S-2) yoyo spin duration (infobox "Spin duration", Yoyos table) has no returning param — it bounds how long a yoyo stays out; (S-3) explosive-aoe carries no ammo class — all launchers are limitations for it; (S-4) whips: Possession "swings four times per use" (no swings-per-use param).

**Sim-side follow-ups**: (a) RESOLVED (AMENDMENTS-013 B64, P3-S9 / P3-S10): summon weapons' `critPct` is not applicable (wiki: summon damage cannot normally crit — absent, not listed: 64 whip / minion / sentry weapons) and the guard does not require it; (b) RESOLVED (P3-S11): thrown explosives' damage / knockback now verify against a second wiki statement (the thrown-explosives paragraph below), and a classless weapon's `critPct` is not applicable (AMENDMENTS-015 B85(b): the wiki Critical hit page — damage of no class "can never deal critical hits"; absent, not listed) and not sim-required (`simRequiredWeaponFields(archetype, damageClass)`; the Lab labels it `classless-crit`); 15 of the 17 thrown explosives are simulated; (c) boomerang / launched-flail cadence: most cannot be re-thrown before returning (Boomerangs article; KO Cannon: "its fire rate is dependent only on the distance to its impact").

**Thrown explosives — blast damage / knockback verification (P3-S11; pipeline/merge/explosives.ts)**: why they were withheld — a thrown explosive's ITEM carries no damage of its own (Module:Iteminfo/data `damage` 0 or −1, `knockBack` 0: the game sets the explosion's damage and knockback on the projectile when it goes off — the Explosives (class) table cites "the Desktop 1.4.5.5 source code, method Kill() in Terraria.Projectile.cs"; Module:Projectileinfo/data has no damage field), so the Iteminfo-vs-infobox cross-check had nothing to compare and both fields stayed Unknown (not a disagreement). Now a value is taken when two independent wiki statements of it agree: the item infobox (Cargo `damage` / `knockback`) AND the item's row of the Explosives (class) types table (rev 1031784; hand-written rows, e.g. Freeze Bomb "0 (to enemies) 1 (to user)"), plus — for damage — the weapon's own article sentence "deals N damage to all …" where it states one (verbatim on the cached revision). Any disagreement withholds the field (source-mismatch gap); a value of 0 stays Unknown. critPct is not applicable (classless, B85(b) — above). The table's "Radius" column (the tile / liquid effect radius from Kill(): Dynamite 7, liquid bombs 3–3.5, Dirt Bomb 4.2) is NOT the damage area and is not used: the blast radius stays the article-stated / B62-inherited / B63 P-5 value (liquid bombs inherit the Bomb's 4-tile damage radius under B62 — recorded here for review).

## Armor — identification and count reconciliation (P2-S8)

| Step | Count |
|---|---:|
| Cargo Items rows whose infobox type contains "armor" | 333 |
| − set rows (type "armor" + "set" = the set infobox of a set article, not an item; 70 set articles) | 77 |
| = armor piece rows | 256 |
| **= armor pieces emitted** | **256** (reconciles) |
|   head / chest / legs | 105 / 81 / 70 |
|   pieces of an emitted set (setId) / without a set | 242 / 14 |
| Module:Iteminfo/data non-vanity items with exactly one head/body/leg slot | 256 |
|   = emitted armor pieces (every emitted piece is one) + Iteminfo-only | 256 + 0 (reconciles) |

**Wiki category tree** — Category:Armor items: 3 categories (Armor items, Armor sets, Armor sets with no set bonus), 97 distinct articles.

| Category-tree articles | Count |
|---|---:|
| articles in the tree | 97 |
| − articles hosting ≥1 emitted armor | 91 |

## Armor sets — set articles, class-variant helmets, set bonuses (P2-S8)

77 Cargo set rows on 70 set articles → **69 armor sets emitted** + 1 withheld by a reviewed, quote-checked pipeline/overlays/armor-sets.json entry (Rain armor; one count-reconciliation gap each, their pieces' setId listed Unknown) (reconciles). Articles with several set rows: Wizard set 8. Validate checks every set as a hard §7a-6 rule: pieces resolve with the matching slot, piece setId back-refs agree (E_DERIVED_MISMATCH), helmets and chests are not repeated and variants ⇄ helmets are bijective — ⇄ helmets × chests where the bonus is keyed by the chest (98 variants = 98 helmet keys). Sets with alternative chests: Beetle armor 2 (bonus keyed by the chest); Wizard set 8; without a legs piece: Wizard set.

Category check: set articles not in a Category:Armor sets* category: none; Category:Armor sets* articles without a Cargo set row: none.

Set Defense totals (infobox, per helmet or one total) vs Σ piece defense (each piece Iteminfo, cross-checked): 79 agree, 0 mismatch, 33 without a total. Variant class (editorialFields) = the damage-class signals of helmet + chest + legs + set bonus stats; hybrid sets are ruled "other" by a quoted overlay entry. Set stage = earliest stage a complete set can be assembled, Unknown unless every piece stage is derived (19/69 derived).

## Equipment effects — closed StatKind classification and Equipinfo cross-check (P2-S8)

Every game-text line (Cargo Items `tooltip` for pieces and accessories, the set infobox "Set Bonus" for sets) matches exactly one rule in pipeline/merge/effects.ts — an unmatched or doubly-matched line fails the build (StatKind is closed). A stat rule's numbers must all occur in the line; specials keep the verbatim line (simulated: false, sourceUrl = the record's article). 107 rules; lines classified: armor pieces + set bonuses 443, accessories 590.

"disagree" withholds the record's effects (Unknown + source-mismatch gap); "partial" = a field stated on one side only: game text only (shared code the wiki leaves unattributed) is kept, Equipinfo only (a stat the game text gives no number for) is a gap and never filled in. Set bonuses have no Equipinfo counterpart (UpdateArmorSet is not transcribed).

## StatKind extension candidates (amendment proposals, P2-S8; B54 landed in the B70 slice)

Effects the closed StatKind enum (DATA_SCHEMA_SPEC §2.3) cannot express are emitted as `special` (simulated: false) — never as an invented stat. These rules name the StatKind extension that would make them structured; each row is wiki evidence (verbatim game text + article) for an amendment. AMENDMENTS-011 B54's 11 kinds are in the enum since the B70 P4-prep slice: their value-stating lines are stats (flip counts in the B54 section below); what remains here are scope / value / unit candidates.

"Existing kind" = the StatKind is in the enum but the game text states no value / unit / scope for it (a unit definition, a value source or a scope extension is the amendment); "new kind" = no StatKind covers it.

|---|---|---:|---|---|

## Tinkering — combinedFrom / upgradesTo derived from Tinkerer's Workshop recipes (§7a-7, P2-S8)

Rule (validate/integrity.ts deriveTinkering, the one definition the build and validate share): a recipe whose station is the Tinkerer's Workshop (item 398) and whose result is an emitted accessory links each ITEM ingredient that is itself an emitted accessory; other stations, group ingredients ("Any …") and materials never link. 151 Tinkerer's Workshop recipes; 137 produce an emitted accessory → 257 combinedFrom links = 257 upgradesTo links (reconciles); 114 accessories have combinedFrom, 167 have upgradesTo. Validate re-derives both directions and fails on any difference (E_DERIVED_MISMATCH) — symmetric by construction and by check.

- Recipes with a group ingredient (the group member is not linked — groups never link): Bundle of Horseshoe Balloons; Bundle of Horseshoe Balloons; Bundle of Horseshoe Balloons
- Two-way links (each is a Tinkerer's Workshop recipe on the wiki; consumers walking the tree must cycle-guard): Band of Starpower ↔ Panic Necklace

## P4-prep gating expansion — deltas (AMENDMENTS-014 B68, AMENDMENTS-015 B78)

Before = the §7b derivation of this same data WITHOUT the new sources (recipes + boss drops only, a vendor route Unknown) — it reproduces the previous report (47/488 weapons, 1097 gating-underived records). After = with the reviewed vendor availability + shop rows (overlays/vendors.json, parse/shops.ts), the container registry (overlays/containers.json), the gatherable base-material registry (overlays/gatherables.json) and the either-or / sufficient-bound rules (merge/gating.ts header).

| accessories | 352 | 23 | 81 | +58 | none |
| ammo items (+ dual-role weapons counted as weapons) | 79 | 2 | 33 | +31 | none |
| potions (new in this slice) | 66 | 0 | 34 | +34 | none |

## P4-prep gating sources — vendors, containers, gatherable materials (B68 / B78)

Vendors (overlays/vendors.json): a purchase route derives only when the NPC's availability is honestly derivable AND the item's shop-table row has a reviewed Availability text; the NPC's own move-in rule is its gate (conditions; "has one of these items" = the minimum over them; "another NPC present").

| NPC | Availability | Shop rows (parsed) | Rows with a reviewed availability | Why |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| [Merchant](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Merchant) | derivable (no progression gate) | 32 | 22 | Coins in the inventory are not a progression gate; the Merchant is available from the start of a world. |
| [Arms Dealer](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Arms_Dealer) | derivable (no progression gate) | 20 | 10 | The article's Spawning criteria list (every name is checked on the article): the Arms Dealer's gate is the minimum over these items' own gating — one of them in the inventory suffices. |
| [Demolitionist](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Demolitionist) | derivable (no progression gate) | 14 | 7 | Spawning criteria list of qualifying explosives (minimum over their gating) and the Merchant's presence. |
| [Clothier](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Clothier) | derivable (post-skeletron) | 60 | 19 | Move-in criterion; post-skeletron maps to the Skeletron node. |
| [Mechanic](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Mechanic) | derivable (post-skeletron) | 33 | 30 | Rescued as the Bound Mechanic, who only appears after Skeletron. |
| [Witch Doctor](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Witch_Doctor) | derivable (post-queen-bee) | 26 | 22 | Move-in criterion; post-queen-bee maps to the Queen Bee node. |
| [Wizard](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Wizard) | derivable (hardmode) | 12 | 11 | The Bound Wizard appears once the Wall of Flesh is defeated = the Hardmode milestone (required-gated by the Wall of Flesh). |
| [Cyborg](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Cyborg) | derivable (post-plantera) | 24 | 14 | Move-in criterion. |
| [Truffle](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Truffle) | derivable (hardmode) | 8 | 4 | The Truffle needs Hardmode; his surface Glowing Mushroom house is a building requirement, not a progression gate. |
| [Goblin Tinkerer](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Goblin_Tinkerer) | derivable (goblin-army-defeated) | 8 | 7 | Rescued as the Bound Goblin after a Goblin Army; the event has no progression edge (overlays/progression.json notes), so the requirement is a condition chip, not a boss gate. |
| [Stylist](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Stylist) | derivable (no progression gate) | 16 | 4 | A rescue in a natural cavern biome: no boss, no Hardmode. |
| [Golfer](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Golfer) | derivable (no progression gate) | 43 | 21 | A rescue in a natural biome: no boss, no Hardmode. |
| [Zoologist](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Zoologist) | derivable (no progression gate) | 40 | 4 | Bestiary completion is filled by meeting creatures, not by a boss or Hardmode. |
| [Skeleton Merchant](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Skeleton_Merchant) | derivable (no progression gate) | 34 | 12 | Appears in the Cavern layer with no boss or Hardmode requirement; his moon-phase stock is mapped per shop row. |
| [Traveling Merchant](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Traveling_Merchant) | not derivable | 126 | — | random visits with a randomized stock — no item is ever guaranteed to be sold |
| [Dryad](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Dryad) | not derivable | 55 | — | moves in after any one of four bosses — an OR the gate set cannot express |
| [Tavernkeep](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Tavernkeep) | not derivable | 42 | — | appears after the Eater of Worlds OR the Brain of Cthulhu — an OR the gate set cannot express |
| [Steampunker](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Steampunker) | not derivable | 38 | — | moves in after any one of the three mechanical bosses — an OR the gate set cannot express |
| [Painter](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Painter) | not derivable | 69 | — | needs a count of other town NPCs, whose own availability differs per NPC |
| [Party Girl](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Party_Girl) | not derivable | 41 | — | needs a count of other town NPCs, whose own availability differs per NPC |
| [Dye Trader](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Dye_Trader) | not derivable | 12 | — | needs a count of other town NPCs, whose own availability differs per NPC |
| [Princess](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Princess) | not derivable | 31 | — | needs every other town NPC present |
| [Santa Claus](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Santa_Claus) | not derivable | 40 | — | needs a completed Frost Legion event (not tracked as a progression gate) during the Christmas season |
| [Pirate](https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Pirate) | not derivable | 10 | — | needs a completed Pirate Invasion (an event marker; event completion is not tracked as a gate) |

## StatKind extension landed (AMENDMENTS-011 B54) — special → stat flips

The 11 pre-approved kinds are in the closed StatKind enum; lines that state their value are stats now (an effect line that also states an unmodeled part keeps that part as a quoted special); lines without a value stay specials ("no value/unit rows stay Unknown"). SCORING_SPEC §2.3 rows: the scoring constants module (src/scoring/constants.ts) does not exist yet — the rows land with it at P4-S1/S2 (constants v1).

## Suggested gear forced by the gating expansion (AMENDMENTS-015 B78 / B83b)

B83(b): gear needs a quoted wiki recommendation AND passing E_SUGGESTION_GATING; a class (or the whole field) goes stale once the boss's own quoted tips name a weapon that passes. The gating expansion made pre-Hardmode weapons pass, so these picks were authored from the bosses' existing quoted tips (overlays/bosses.json); classes whose tips name no passing weapon stay Unknown with their reason.

## Fishing routes (AMENDMENTS-004 B20)

Source: the "Fishing catches" article (https://terraria.wiki.gg/wiki/Fishing_catches, rev 1031711) — 32 condition definitions, 150 catch rows (133 with items), 128 distinct items. Skipped: NPC catch (no item) 5, Remix special seed only (seed worlds not modeled) 12.

Records with a fishing route: 128 — weapons 9, armor 0, accessories 2, items 117. Routes with Unknown conditions (an unmapped label): 6 records; unmapped labels: "Junk" 2, "No Advanced Combat Techniques" 1, ">1000 tiles of liquid" 1.

Gating: a fishing route never derives a gate (blocker fishing-route-ungated). Records whose gatedBy changed when fishing joined the deriver: none (every fished record was already Unknown, or has a gate-free route).

Tagged as fished by the wiki but not in any catch table (gap each, acquisition hint): none.

## Pipeline next-touch queue closed by P6-S0

- **Skeletron's Hellforge caption (AMENDMENTS-015 B84):** condition hellforge-absent ("Hellforge not present", conditions overlay) matches both the drop-list caption "When Hellforge is not present" and the Cargo note "when a Hellforge is not present; see conditions". Skeletron → Hellforge now: 221-skeletron-skeletron-head-cf31ecd5 conditions [hellforge-absent].
- **"Drops together" linkage (B84 / B66 candidate):** `DropSource.dropsWith` (additive, symmetric, validated) on 24 records of the bundled cells (Shadow Orb: i:96 ↔ 97-shadow-orb-946bd524; Shadow Orb: i:96 ↔ 97-shadow-orb-eac69fde; Crimson Heart: i:97 ↔ 800-crimson-heart-946bd524; Crimson Heart: i:97 ↔ 800-crimson-heart-eac69fde; Shadow Orb: i:97 ↔ 96-shadow-orb-946bd524; Shadow Orb: i:97 ↔ 96-shadow-orb-eac69fde; Plantera (Second Form): i:758 ↔ 771-plantera-second-form-741638a5; Treasure Bag (Plantera): i:758 ↔ 771-treasure-bag-plantera-741638a5; Plantera (Second Form): i:771 ↔ 758-plantera-second-form-741638a5; Treasure Bag (Plantera): i:771 ↔ 758-treasure-bag-plantera-741638a5; Crimson Heart: i:800 ↔ 97-crimson-heart-946bd524; Crimson Heart: i:800 ↔ 97-crimson-heart-eac69fde; Golem: i:1258 ↔ 1261-golem-741638a5; Treasure Bag (Golem): i:1258 ↔ 1261-treasure-bag-golem-741638a5; Golem: i:1261 ↔ 1258-golem-741638a5; Treasure Bag (Golem): i:1261 ↔ 1258-treasure-bag-golem-741638a5; Pumpking: i:1782 ↔ 1783-pumpking-741638a5; Pumpking: i:1783 ↔ 1782-pumpking-741638a5; Pumpking: i:1784 ↔ 1785-pumpking-741638a5; Pumpking: i:1785 ↔ 1784-pumpking-741638a5; … (+4)).
- **Boss stats parse reasons (B83(f)):** wholly Unknown stats from unparseable health / defense cells now carry their reason as a missing-field gap on statsByDifficulty (the page's "Stats not verified" line shows it): Brain of Cthulhu: No single verified value per difficulty in the wiki's infobox: health is flagged by the wiki as based on outdated source code; Queen Bee: No single verified value per difficulty in the wiki's infobox: a defense value carries a note; Skeletron: No single verified value per difficulty in the wiki's infobox: defense has several values (alternatives on further lines); Skeletron Prime: No single verified value per difficulty in the wiki's infobox: defense has several values (alternatives on further lines); Duke Fishron: No single verified value per difficulty in the wiki's infobox: defense has several values (alternatives on further lines); Lunatic Cultist: No single verified value per difficulty in the wiki's infobox: defense has text next to the value ("/ 27").
- **Mechanical bosses quote (B93(ii)):** the three mech → all-mechanical-bosses edges now quote "One Plantera's Bulb immediately spawns once all three mechanical bosses are defeated for the first time." (a whole sentence; the display-side CUT_AT_END pin no longer matches any edge).
- **Special:Search links (B96(d)):** items.detail.json now also holds every item a boss record references (summon + prep-checklist items, 30 corpus records: Torch; Suspicious Looking Eye; Magic Mirror; Worm Food; Grappling Hook; Wood Platform; Bed; Mechanical Eye; Mechanical Worm; Mechanical Skull; Slime Crown; Campfire; Abeemination; Life Fruit; Lihzahrd Power Cell; Rod of Discord; Bloody Spine; Eater of Souls Banner; Flying Snake Banner; Lihzahrd Banner; Blue Slime Banner; Heart Lantern; Bug Net; Recall Potion; Truffle Worm; Peace Candle; Celestial Sigil; Prismatic Lacewing; Gelatin Crystal; Deer Thing), so the boss pages and the readiness checker resolve them to their records — the Suspicious Looking Eye included — instead of the wiki search.

- **Boss-page drop rows (AMENDMENTS-016 B104):** items.detail.json also holds every corpus item a boss page lists as a drop (Boss.dropIds; 169 records, e.g. Demonite Ore; Corrupt Seeds; Shadow Scale; Blue Brick; Green Brick; Pink Brick; Hellforge; Pwnhammer; Soul of Fright; Soul of Might; Soul of Sight; Bee Hat; … (+157)), so the boss pages resolve each drop row to its record (its own item page) instead of the wiki search. The core file now holds 344 records.

**No-stated-quantity ruling (AMENDMENTS-016 B105, applied by pipeline/merge/quantity.ts).** 75 drop records state no quantity on the wiki. 27 of them — non-consumable (Module:Iteminfo/data consumable false) weapons, armor pieces, accessories and vanity / tool / pet / mount items — now carry quantity 1, derived from the item kind and listed in `_prov.modeledFields` ("quantity: 1 derived from non-consumable item kind"; validate re-checks each, the drop tables label the value). ## Data file growth watch (AMENDMENTS-016 B104)

stage-gaps.json: 1824 entries, 29.3 KB gz (budget 300 KB gz). The app loads it only on boss pages (optional: the stated reasons behind declared Unknowns), so its growth with the item corpus costs those pages alone. Entries by ref kind: i 1699, drop 61, boss 52, rcp 8, grp 3, set 1. When it nears the budget, split it by ref kind (boss pages read only boss: / drop: / i: reasons of their own records) the way the item details and the sprite manifest are split.
items.detail.json: 344 records, 25.5 KB gz (Lab, boss pages and readiness load it whole); items.list.json: 114.9 KB gz (the encyclopedia browse); search-docs.json: 115.1 KB gz (now with each record's sprite path).

## Sprites

public/sprites/manifest.json lists 488/488 weapon sprites, 18/18 boss sprites, 79/79 ammo item sprites, 66/66 potion sprites, 256/256 armor piece sprites and 352/352 accessory sprites (28 animated GIFs), each with its wiki File: page, SHA-1, size and rights "re-logic"; 0 weapons, 0 armor pieces and 0 accessories have no wiki image and use the placeholder. Binaries are downloaded locally by `npm run pipeline:sprites` (verified against imageinfo — exact SHA-1 or CDN original size, see #8; gitignored, never in CI — A10); fallbackMode=false.

Thumbnails (AMENDMENTS-007 B30): every sprite file over 100 KB gets a static PNG of its first frame, downscaled by a whole number to ≤ 128 px (nearest-neighbour block sampling — pixel art is never resampled fractionally), generated locally by `npm run pipeline:sprites`: /sprites/bosses/113.gif (boss:wall-of-flesh, 668777 B, 210×420) → /sprites/thumbs/bosses/113.png 53×105; /sprites/bosses/396.gif (boss:moon-lord, 427536 B, 191×150) → /sprites/thumbs/bosses/396.png 96×75; /sprites/bosses/636.gif (boss:empress-of-light, 677616 B, 386×218) → /sprites/thumbs/bosses/636.png 97×55. Heavy files without a thumbnail (generator decodes GIF only): none. Rows and cards should use the thumbnail, the full file only on detail / boss pages (UI wiring: next UI slice).

Runtime split (AMENDMENTS-016 B104): the app no longer fetches manifest.json (270.5 KB gz, kept as the committed provenance record). It reads manifest.index.json (509 B gz: the fallbackMode lever + 11 parts) and loads only the parts holding the sprites a page draws: manifest.accessories.json (352 files, 5.8 KB gz); manifest.armor.json (256 files, 3.4 KB gz); manifest.bosses.json (18 files, 0.7 KB gz); manifest.items.0.json (655 files, 8.5 KB gz); manifest.items.1.json (822 files, 11.6 KB gz); manifest.items.2.json (915 files, 11.9 KB gz); manifest.items.3.json (737 files, 10.7 KB gz); manifest.items.4.json (872 files, 12.5 KB gz); manifest.items.5.json (879 files, 12.7 KB gz); manifest.items.6.json (137 files, 1.8 KB gz); manifest.weapons.json (488 files, 7.7 KB gz).

## TO-VERIFY-IN-P2 resolutions

- **#4 NPCs table per-difficulty hp/defense** — Cargo NPCs `life` / `defense` / `damage` are rendered infobox cells: a per-difficulty block (`npcstat` or `mode-content` with m-normal / m-expert / m-master / m-expert-master / m-all spans), or a bare integer for every difficulty (6 of 1,538 cells). Additional lines are alternative values (enraged, per-health, with minions) or — after a "(contact)" note — projectile damages; cells can carry the wiki's "based on outdated versions of Terraria's source code" category flag. pipeline/parse/npcStats.ts keeps only one integer per difficulty and withholds everything else; a boss difficulty is emitted only with both hp and defense verified (6/18 bosses).
- **#1 Cargo Items columns / class / rarity** — Cargo Items has 43 columns (itemid, name, internalname, image, imagefile, imageplaced, imageequipped, autoswing, stack, consumable, hardmode, type, listcat, tag, damage, damagetype, defense, velocity, knockback, research, rare, buy, sell, axe, pick, hammer, fishing, bait, bonus, toolspeed, usetime, unobtainable, critical, tooltip, placeable, placedwidth, placedheight, mana, hheal, mheal, bodyslot, buffs, debuffs). `type` is a ^-list of infobox types ("weapon", "tool", "ammunition", …); `damagetype` is the class as text (Melee/Ranged/Magic/Summon, empty for classless) and agrees with the Iteminfo class flags for every identified weapon (455 agree, 0 mismatch, 33 with no wiki class: 32 classless in both sources, 1 with an Iteminfo class only); `rare` is the game rarity number (plain or wrapped in the rarity template "Rarity level: N"; 488/488 weapons agree with Iteminfo); `critical` = 4 + Iteminfo crit (391 agree, 0 mismatch); `usetime` = Iteminfo useAnimation; `velocity` is the effective projectile velocity (equals Iteminfo shootSpeed for 330 weapons, differs for 95 — extra updates / ammo), so it is not cross-checked. `tag` carries vendor:<NPC> (purchase routes), `listcat` route hints; `stack` reports 9999 for weapons (B7 — maxStack only for consumable weapons, from Iteminfo).
- **#9 special rarity tiers** — Module:Iteminfo/data `rare`: -1 gray, 0–11 numeric tiers, -11 amber (quest fish + Strange Plants), -12 rainbow (Expert items; flag expert:true, base tier in `rarity`), -13 fiery red (Master items). Cargo `rare` uses the same numbers (Shield of Cthulhu -12, Eye of Cthulhu Relic -13). Mapped to Rarity -1..11 | "amber" | "rainbow" | "fiery-red"; 0 weapons use a special tier (weapon rarities span 0–10).
- **#10 useTime vs useAnimation** — The wiki's "Use time" equals Iteminfo useAnimation for 488/488 weapons with a wiki value (useTime differs from the wiki value for 42, e.g. Zenith useTime 10 / useAnimation 30). useAnimation = length of one use (attack cycle); useTime = interval between successive uses/shots within it (bursts, multi-swing projectiles). Emitted: useTimeTicks = useTime, useAnimationTicks = useAnimation (both always present). Display "Use time" from useAnimationTicks (wiki parity); attack cycles per second = 60 / useAnimationTicks; useAnimationTicks was added to the weapons list projection for that reason.
- **#11 ammo damage / velocity modifier location (RESOLVED, P2-S7)** — A weapon's ammo class is Iteminfo `useAmmo` = the game's AmmoID, i.e. the item ID of the class's representative item (40 Wooden Arrow, 97 Musket Ball, 771 Rocket I, 23 Gel …); ammo items carry the same value in `ammo` plus their own `damage`, `knockBack` and `shootSpeed`, which the game adds to the weapon's (Cargo Items `damage` / `knockback` / `velocity` = the same base values: 64 / 59 / 51 ammo records agree, 0 mismatches). ammoClass = slug of the representative item's name (17 classes among weapons: acorn, ale, candy-corn, copper-coin, explosive-jack-o-lantern, fallen-star, flare, gel, musket-ball, nail, rocket-i, sand-block, seed, snowball, stake, stynger-bolt, wooden-arrow; every one matched by ≥1 ammo record — §7a-10 is a hard check again). The velocity MULTIPLIER (B2) is the ammo projectile's `extraUpdates` + 1: Module:Projectileinfo/data rev 1030091 (generated 2026-09-13 15:45 (+00:00)) carries extraUpdates per projectile, and the wiki's item infobox shows "Velocity Multiplier N×" (linked to Velocity#extraUpdates) — emitted from the item's own infobox, cross-checked with Projectileinfo (42 agree, 0 mismatch). Iteminfo `shoot` is NOT always a projectile for ammo (rockets and solutions store a type index: Rocket II "shoots" projectile 3), so Projectileinfo is never joined without the infobox naming the projectile. Per-weapon ammo conversions (Uzi → High Velocity Bullets, …) are weapon behaviors (P3 archetype params).
- **#12 structured buff-ID source (RESOLVED, P2-S7)** — The wiki article "Buff IDs" (rev 1029302) is a table of every game buff: ID, name, internal name and type (Buff / Debuff), each name linking its article (352 named rows; potion buffs link their potion's page, e.g. Ironskin → Ironskin Potion). Buff articles repeat the ID in their infobox ("Buff ID" on buff pages, "Internal Buff ID" on potion/weapon pages) — 77 agree, 0 mismatch, 0 articles state none. Iteminfo `buffType` (the buff an item grants) is the same ID (Ale → 25 Tipsy). One name can cover several IDs (Zapped 399 / 400) → gameBuffId Unknown. Debuff articles state damage over time in their infobox "Effects" row per target ("−24 health per second (on enemies)" / "(on players)"; unqualified = every entity) → Buff.dot.dps is the enemy value.
- **D1 caveat: 1.4.5.7 → 1.4.5.8 patch diff** — The 1.4.5.8 patch notes (wiki page "1.4.5.8", rev 1029883) name three weapons — Clay Bud Staff (now always knocks enemies away), Last Prism (laser fades without enough mana), Acorn Slingshot (crash fix) — all behavioral; no numeric item stat changed, so no extracted field is in the patch-diff set. The Cargo cross-check above is the re-confirmation for every compared field.
- **critPct for summon and classless weapons** — The wiki's Critical hit page (rev 1032172) states summon damage cannot normally deal critical hits, and the infobox shows no crit for all 64 summon weapons; critPct is therefore not applicable (absent, not listed). The same page states damage of no class "can never deal critical hits" (AMENDMENTS-015 B85(b), superseding B17's Unknown): classless weapons' critPct is not applicable too — the infobox shows no crit for 32 of the 32 classless weapons.
- **#5 Equipinfo columns (defense, tooltip, wing flight time) (RESOLVED, P2-S8)** — Cargo Equipinfo (184 columns, 503 rows on Template:GetEquipInfo/db) is the wiki's transcription of the game's UpdateEquip effect code per item: class damage as fractions (meleeDamage "+0.1"), crit / mana / minion slots as integers, moveSpeed, endurance, pickSpeed, whipRangeMultiplier, flags (noKnockback, fireWalk, buffImmune lists, accRunSpeed …). It has NO defense, tooltip or wing flight-time column: defense comes from Module:Iteminfo/data `defense` (cross-checked with the Cargo Items infobox: armor 253 agree / 0 mismatch; accessories 16 / 0), the tooltip from Cargo Items `tooltip` (game text), and flight time from the "Wings" article list table (Iteminfo `wingSlot` only names the wing). 19 Equipinfo rows carry no item ID (effect code shared by several items). Equipinfo is therefore the cross-check of the tooltip-derived stats, not their source.
- **#2 recipe-group encoding** — Cargo Recipes stores a group ingredient as its plain English name ("¦Any Iron Bar¦" in `ingredients`, "Any Iron Bar¦2" in `args`, "¦Any Iron Bar¦2" in `ings`); members are not in Cargo. Members come from Module:Recipes `group("Any …", {names})` (what the {{recipes}} template uses), resolved to IDs via Module:Iteminfo/data and cross-checked against Module:RecipeGroups (the game's SetupRecipeGroups, v1.4.5.4 transcription). Station alternatives (B3) come from Module:Recipes/l10n `en.station_cate` ("Iron Anvil" → "Iron or Lead Anvil").
- **#3 drop NPC ids / multi-part bosses** — Cargo Drops `id` carries NPC ids for most NPC rows (single, comma lists, ranges, per-variant notes, negative variant netIDs); container rows (crates, chests, treasure bags) carry none or the boss id. Ids are cross-checked against Cargo NPCs by name (mismatches withheld + gap); empty ids are filled from NPCs. Multi-part bosses: every part/segment NPC is typed `boss Part` and lives on the boss article (`_page`), so drops attribute to that boss. P2-S6: the boss registry resolves each boss-typed / treasure-bag row by its article (bossId, cross-checked against the boss's NPC ids); rows on event-boss articles get no bossId (not applicable — events are markers, A3).
- **#8 Imageinfo → sprite file resolution** — Cargo Items `imagefile` names the infobox image ("Zenith.png"); `prop=imageinfo&iiprop=url|size|sha1|mime` on "File:<imagefile>" (50 titles per request) returns the original file URL on terraria.wiki.gg/images/, its SHA-1, size and dimensions; the URL's ?<6 hex> key is the SHA-1 prefix (pins the file version). The wiki's CDN (Cloudflare Polish, `cf-polished: ok, orig_size=N`) serves most PNGs re-encoded, so a byte-exact SHA-1 match is only possible for some files; downloads are accepted when the SHA-1 matches exactly OR the CDN-reported original size equals the wiki's recorded size of that version (pipeline:sprites reports both counts).
- **#13 Cargo paging** — limit=5000 requested; the server returned at most 500 rows per page for every table (Recipes 9 pages, Drops 7, NPCs 2, Items 13, Weapon_source 3). Pagination is by offset ordered by _ID until COUNT(*) is reached.
